Message from the President




                                                                                             Elevating the Shimizu Brand for Long-Term
                                                                                             Growth and Value Creation



                                                                                             President and Representative Director




                                                                       Top Message
                                                                                                                                     Our core principles, rooted in our founding spirit and the principles of
                                                                                                                                     The Analects and the Abacus, and the Shimizu brand we have accumu-
                                                                                                                                     lated over our long history, form the foundation of the Group’s sustained
                                                                                                                                     competitive advantage over our peers. Guided by these immutable val-
                                                                                                                                     ues passed down over 220 years, we aim to sustain company growth
                                                                                                                                     and increase corporate value over the long term through disciplined
                                                                                                                                     efforts to improve capital efficiency, strengthen corporate governance,
                                                                                                                                     and pursue growth-oriented investments.

In 2026, two M&A transactions were officially approved by the exec-
utive team and the Board of Directors following extensive deliberation
and vetting. The first, enacted in March, brought AOMI Construction
Co., Ltd. into our Group. Primarily engaged in marine and land-based
civil engineering and ground improvement, AOMI Construction boasts
technological excellence and a formidable track record in each area.
The second transaction welcomed American Engineering Corporation
(Okinawa). The company specializes in construction for US military




10         Shimizu Corporate Report 2026

Special
Contents        1
      Passing on Technical Skills
      and Developing Talent

                Nobuyuki Yamamoto                                 Tatsuya Shimmura
                General Master Carpenter in the Project
 Conversation   (to reconstruct Shurijo Castle’s main hall)       President and Representative
                Chairman and Representative Director of           Director of Shimizu Corporation
                Shajiken Co., Ltd.


As part of our efforts to sustain the Shimizu Group’s business growth, we go back to
our origins—being meticulous about quality, safety, cost, and process—in order to
establish Shimizu as a brand of Monozukuri trusted by clients.
Here, Tatsuya Shimmura talks with Nobuyuki Yamamoto, a shrine architect and torch-
bearer of traditional Japanese architectural techniques. As fellow travelers on the road
of Monozukuri, Yamamoto and Shimmura discuss each other’s foundational inspira-
tions and other topics such as passing on expertise and nurturing talent.


                                                                                                                                      A conversation held at the Former Shibusawa Residence within our Smart Innovation Ecosystem NOVARE       Former Shibusawa Residence




Making a proud example of Reiwa architecture                                                  Yamamoto In our work, we use technology that has been handed                             project to reconstruct the castle, they generally use computer-aided
                                                                                              down over more than a millennium, and the examples we refer to are                       architectural design (CAD). They also scan the property with a laser

Shimmura At the end of the day, companies and construction
sites are made up of people. Handing down expertise and nurturing
talent are the two core pillars of our company. However, specialized
knowledge and skills are not the only things you need to transfer
to the next generation of talent. You also need to pass down how
to communicate effectively on the job, how to relate to people, and
what mindset you require.
On the matter of communication, while it is important to express
yourself clearly, it is also important to ask about or anticipate what
other people need or what they are thinking. Communication is not
one-way. You must place yourself in the other person’s position and
keep a listening ear.

Grounded in tradition, forging the future

Shimmura Since becoming President, I have promoted the slo-
gan: “establishing the Shimizu brand by going back to the origins.”
Going back to the origins does not mean going back to the past. It
is a future-oriented process in which we update our 220-year-old
heritage by blending it with the technology of today. Our goal should
be to retain the trust of clients by remaining as meticulous as ever
about quality, safety, cost, process, and environment. My mission is

Transcending National and Business Boundaries                                                                          The Optimal Solution for BIM Production that Balances Quality and Competitiveness

Shimizu North America (SNA) is a U.S.-based company specializing exclusively in the Construction Business, and         BIM is a methodology that integrates building information digitally and seamlessly connects the design, construc-
the Engineering Headquarters operates its plant business primarily in Japan. The “Q&B Foods Tennessee Factory          tion, and operation and maintenance processes. The use of BIM is widely established in Asia and other overseas
Construction Project” is a prime example of how combining the strengths of these two entities enabled us to meet       markets. Our global operations have honed our technical expertise by adapting to the advanced BIM requirements
our client’s expectations. From the design phase onward, Japan and the U.S. worked together, leveraging their          demanded in such environments. In 2021, we established a BIM Support Center in Manila, which now provides
respective strengths to carry out the project.                                                                         robust support for BIM operations at our regional offices around the world.
SNA secured the contract for this project, while the Engineering Headquarters provided technical support for the       In recent years, we have expanded the scope of the center’s operations to include our domestic operations
process EPC (plant engineering) work. The BIM platform served as the backbone of this collaboration. By utilizing      in Japan. Collaboration with Manila enables us to achieve a high-level balance between the quality standards
3D models, we comprehensively coordinated the building, MEP services, production equipment, and process piping.        we uphold and cost competitiveness, while also addressing the workforce shortage in Japan and supporting a

Business Overview
To adapt flexibly to changes in the social environment and pursue dynamic business growth, we are actively implementing strategies tailored to the unique characteristics of our seven businesses in the two fields of
Construction and Real Estate and Other. We continue to actively implement strategies tailored to the business environment and business characteristics of the moment in order to firmly meet the needs of our clients while
fulfilling our social responsibility as a sustainable company.



    Consolidated                                                                                                                                                 We are engaged in the planning, design, construction, operation, and maintenance of a wide range

    Building Construction

    T O P I C S

    The Massive Steel Frame Supporting Japan’s Tallest Super-High-Rise Building                                                                                                                                Design and Construction Techniques That
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Support High-Quality Spatial Value
    At “Torch Tower” (approximately 385 meters tall)—a high-rise building set to become Japan’s tallest upon completion—the steel framing for the “diagrid
    structure,” characterized by massive diagonal steel columns that define the facade* of the lower sections, was completed in January 2026. This is a                                                        “TOKYO DREAM PARK,” led by TV Asahi Corporation and designed and
    key component of the “shell-damped structure” structural design developed by Mitsubishi Jisho Design Inc., and its realization was made possible by                                                        constructed by our company, is a multi-purpose entertainment complex
    our company’s technical developments aimed at bringing the design to life.                                                                                                                                 that offers a diverse range of experiential values, including a concert
    The diagrid framework, which extends continuously up to the 9th-floor level                                                                                                                                hall, theater, and art spaces.
    (52 meters high), has a total steel weight of approximately 11,000 metric                                                                                                                                  The concert hall features high-performance audio equipment to deliver
    tons, making it one of the largest building structures in Japan. Currently, the                                                                                                                            a premium sound experience, and incorporates the “Dynamic Live Floor”
    project is progressing steadily toward completion, not only through rigorous                                                                                                                               vibration-damping system to prevent floor vibrations—common during
    safety and quality control but also by boldly tackling challenges unique to su-                                                                                                                            live events—from being transmitted to the building’s foundation or the
    per-tall high-rise buildings, such as optimizing the flow of people and mate-                                                                                                                              underlying ground.
    rials. Through the creation of this new landmark, our company will cultivate                                                                                                                               Furthermore, we built a BIM-centered digital platform to simulate
    cutting-edge expertise and apply it to the creation of next-generation cities.                                                                                                                             stage visibility from each seat in the theater, facilitating smooth con-
    * Exterior design as seen from the front of the building                                                                                                                                                   sensus-building among stakeholders and streamlining the planning of

                                                                                                                                                                                                               temporary work, material transport, and construction procedures during
                                                                                                                                                                                                               the project.
              VOICE                                                                                                                                                                                            Moving forward, we will continue to enhance the value of spaces
                                                                                                                                                                                                               through both design and construction by leveraging technological ad-
            Shimizu is mobilizing its full range of capabilities to advance the construction of Torch Tower                                                                                                    vancements, bringing to life architecture that inspires people.

            Torch Tower is a building that presents significant construction challenges. To achieve a height of 385
            meters in earthquake-prone Japan, it employs a diagrid framework up to the 9th floor and an outer-braced
            structure* from the 9th to the 56th floor.
            By pooling the strengths of various divisions—including design and production engineering—and lever-
            aging Shimizu’s comprehensive capabilities, we are moving the project forward while resolving numer-
            ous challenges. Regarding the diagrid framework—which was the greatest challenge—we conducted a                                                                Naoki Takehara
            mock-up prior to construction to identify the final remaining issues and establish clear countermeasures.                                                TOKIWABASHI PROJECT Office
                                                                                                                                                                           Project Director
            Thanks to these efforts, the steel framing for the low-rise section was completed two weeks ahead of
            schedule, and we have received praise for Shimizu’s technical expertise from the client and the architec-
            tural construction administrator.
            We will continue to work together toward the scheduled completion in 2028.
            * A structure that enhances seismic and wind resistance through bracing (diagonal members) installed around the perimeter of the building

                                                                                                                                                                                                               Inside TOKYO DREAM PARK SGC HALL ARIAKE (Koto Ward, Tokyo)

                      Civil Engineering
                      In the Civil Engineering Business, we contribute to the realization of safe, secure, and abundant living for everyone
                      through the development of social infrastructure such as roads, railways, and water and sewer systems.

                      We will address society’s demands for disaster prevention and mitigation, as well as national resilience, in response to the increasing
                      frequency and severity of natural disasters. At the same time, we will appropriately respond to the dynamic changes in the envi-
                      ronment surrounding the Civil Engineering Business, including measures to manage aging infrastructure and initiatives to achieve                                                                                              Takefumi Saito
                      carbon neutrality. We will build a stable revenue base and develop social infrastructure under a system of reliable quality control and                                                                                       Representative Director
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    Executive Vice President and Executive Officer
                      safety management. We will strive to meet the construction needs of society and our clients and solve their problems by developing                                                                                            Director, Civil Engineering Headquarters

                      and implementing new technologies and expanding our business into new fields.

                    Overseas Construction
                    By pooling the group’s comprehensive strengths, we provide swift and precise solutions
                    to meet the needs of our global clients and address the challenges faced by local communities

                    We have been involved in a wide variety of projects around the world. Currently, we are expanding our operations primarily in Asia,
                    North America, and Africa. While we are working to establish a structure that enables each regional office to conduct business au-

Investment and Development (Real Estate Development)
To respond flexibly to the changing business environment, we will advance a real estate development business
that generates stable revenue through both leasing and sales. We aim to build an optimal portfolio by leverag-
ing the technology and collective strength of the Shimizu Group.
Aiming to establish a real estate value chain and expand our stock business through group collaboration, Shimizu Real Estate Asset
Management Corporation (SRA) has been actively managing private REITs and private funds, and its asset portfolio is becoming
increasingly diversified. We will continue to prioritize sustainability management in our business planning while also pursuing new
growth fields and reforming our business promotion structure to expand our businesses and enhance our earnings power.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        Kounari Yotsumoto
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        Executive Officer
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        Director, Investment and Development Div.

                          Engineering
                          We support the creation of a zero-carbon society and the development of safe, secure, and healthy living en-
                          vironments through engineering. This includes GX using renewable energy facilities and digital transformation
                          (DX) through the shift to smart buildings and towns.
                          In our Engineering Business, we aim to strengthen our competitiveness and stabilize our earnings through engineering capabilities
                          that accurately address the increasingly sophisticated, complex, and diverse needs of society across our four core fields: “Plant
                          Engineering,” “ICT Solutions,” “Soil Remediation,” and “New Energy Engineering.” Furthermore, by closely monitoring new markets                                                                                                                    Yutaka Shimizu
                          emerging from emerging social challenges and continuously pursuing opportunities in promising growth areas, we are committed to                                                                                                                    Managing Officer
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             Director, Engineering Headquarters

                      Group Management
                      We intend to build a Group management structure that is both flexible and fast-moving.

                      The Shimizu Group has 130 subsidiaries, including overseas entities, and 37 affiliated companies*. These companies operate various businesses,
                      such as construction, real estate development, engineering, and green energy development.
                      While keeping the possibility of capital alliances and M&A in mind, we aim to strengthen our core management foundations—our talent and
                      organizational capabilities—and maximize synergies across the Group. Furthermore, by fostering a sense of unity throughout the Group based on
                      smooth communication, we will work to enhance the Group’s overall capabilities and achieve sustainable growth.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    Takao Haneda
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    Executive Vice President and
                      * As of June 30, 2026                                                                                                                                                                                                         Executive Officer
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    In charge of Group Companies

       M&A and Alliances


M&A Strategy and Approach                                                                                                                                               Initiatives to Foster Collaboration and
                                                                                                                                                                        Synergy with Newly Joined Group Companies
With the aim of achieving the goals set forth in “SHIMZ VISION 2030” and the “Mid-Term Business Plan       〈2024-2026〉     ,” we
view M&A as one of the key means of realizing growth and are committed to actively utilizing it. Provided that such transactions                                        For companies newly joining the Shimizu Group, we aim to enhance each company’s corporate
contribute to achieving our goals and realizing growth, we are pursuing M&A opportunities, primarily targeting companies that                                           value and maximize the value we provide to our clients through group-wide collaboration and
will strengthen the competitiveness of our existing businesses or expand into new business areas.                                                                       cooperation, while leveraging each company’s unique characteristics and strengths.
In advancing M&A, we consider it essential that the target company’s business strategy and direction align with ours and that                                           To ensure smooth communication with each company, we will establish a multi-layered cooperative
synergies—in terms of technology, client base, and know-how—can be expected with the Shimizu Group. Focusing primarily                                                  framework spanning from the front lines of operations to executive management. Building on this
on companies that are highly compatible with the businesses of existing Group companies, we will evaluate and make decisions                                            foundation, we will promote the sharing of technical and business know-how as well as the flexi-
on a project-by-project basis from a comprehensive perspective, aiming for sustainable growth while maintaining a sense of                                              ble utilization of talent. Furthermore, by deepening collaboration and information sharing within the
unity within the Group.                                                                                                                                                 Group and implementing policies and initiatives that lead to increased orders, cost reductions, and
                                                                                                                                                                        enhanced technical capabilities, we will strive to maximize synergies with our new Group companies.

                      Group Management

                      T O P I C S (Value Creation Through Group Collaboration)

                      Through strong collaboration between Shimizu Corporation and its Group companies, the Shimizu Group generates synergies across a wide range of fields, maximizing the value we provide to our clients.
                      We will continue to further strengthen this collaboration and meet our clients’ expectations by fully leveraging each company’s expertise and strengths.

Human Capital



    Diversity, Equity & Inclusion (DE&I)
                                                                                                                               Employment and Active Advancement of People with Disabilities
  Policy and Commitment
                                                                                                                               Our company aims to create an environment where employees with disabilities can thrive in their work, and since

   Intellectual Property

  Policy and Commitment                                                                                                                          Open Licensing of Technology
                                                                                                                                                 In August 2025, as a first-of-its-kind initiative in the construction industry, we opened up access to over 200 of
The construction industry is built on a foundation of technologies developed to meet a wide variety of client
                                                                                                                                                 our construction-related patented technologies to external parties. We have systematically made these patent-
needs. It operates in an extremely competitive environment where a broad range of specialized technologies are
                                                                                                                                                 ed technologies public on our corporate website and established a system that makes them easily accessible
integrated in sophisticated and complex ways. In this context, differentiating ourselves through technical expertise
                                                                                                                                                 to many construction-related companies—including our industry peers—by setting low, fixed annual licensing
is essential to achieving sustainable growth and ensuring that clients continue to choose us. We view intellectual
                                                                                                                                                 fees. The purpose of this initiative is not to increase revenue through licensing, but to promote the mutual use of
property not merely as a collection of rights, but as a “strategic asset” for building competitive advantage, and we
                                                                                                                                                 patented technologies.
are promoting an intellectual property strategy closely aligned with our technology strategy.

                                                                                                                                                 similar technologies” as part of its strategy to compete for orders received—has been a major challenge. Through
  Management System                                                                                                                              this initiative, we aim to curb duplicate investment by promoting the mutual use of technologies within the in-

cross-functional collaboration through digital transformation (DX). Revamp” to unify data flows across the organization and “Management with the Maximum Use of Data” by                                                                                                                         launched the “Shimizu Digital Academy” and rolled out a training program to nurture DX Specialists responsible
integrating operational data with market data.           Social and Relational Capital



             Human Rights Initiatives                                                                                                       Community Partnerships

            Policy and Commitment                                                                                                          Policy and Commitment

          The Shimizu Group works to establish a corporate culture that respects human rights. We do this to ensure                       To coexist with society, we not only build and maintain positive relationships with local communities but also
          that each and every officer and employee respects diversity, unique character, and individuality, and does not                  actively promote social contribution activities. Overseas, we not only comply with international rules and local laws
          discriminate on the basis of ethnicity, religion, nationality, age, sex, gender identity, sexual orientation, or disability.    but also respect the culture and customs of each country and strive to contribute to local development.
          Furthermore, our officers and employees do not engage in harassment that damages the dignity of any individual,                 Furthermore, in the event of natural disasters such as major earthquakes, we carry out disaster response activi-
          and do not tolerate such acts by others. We also respect the freedom of association and the right to engage in                  ties promptly and in an organized manner to fulfill our corporate social mission and role and contribute to society.
          collective bargaining, and prohibit child labor and forced labor in any form.

       Decarbonization                                                                                                               Material Circulation

      Policy and Commitment                                                                                                         Policy and Commitment

    The Shimizu Group has established greenhouse gas emissions reduction targets aligned with the 1.5°C level to                  To date, we have worked to conserve resources and reduce and recycle construction by-products by promoting
    achieve carbon neutrality by 2050. Based on these targets, we will not only reduce Scope 1 and Scope 2 emis-                  4R initiatives in our construction business activities. In our Resource Circulation Roadmap, in addition to the “end-
    sions but will also actively work to reduce Scope 3 emissions throughout the supply chain.                                    of-life” target of achieving a 0% final disposal rate for construction by-products by 2050, we have established the
                                                                                                                                  “Input-side Recycling Utilization Rate*” as a new management target, conducting target setting for specific goals
      Initiatives                                                                                                                 of at least 25% by 2030 and at least 50% by 2050.

    Expanding the Adoption at Construction Sites for Biofuels
    As an initiative to reduce CO2 emissions at construction sites, we                = Construction Sites
                                                                                        Using Biofuels
    are promoting the on-site adoption of biofuels. Until now, adoption
    had been limited to construction sites in the Tokyo metropolitan
    area, where subsidy programs were underway; however, the use of
    biofuels is now expanding to construction sites across the country.




    SUSMICS-C Environmentally Friendly Concrete
    To achieve carbon neutrality, we have developed “SUSMICS-C,” an environmentally friendly concrete blended
    with biochar produced by carbonizing woody biomass. As an example of this technology’s application, we poured                                                                                                                                     Amount of Construction Materials Delivered
    100 cubic meters of this concrete into the structural frame of an aggregate washing plant—a temporary struc-                                                                                  Amount of Recycling Utilization                           × Recycling Utilization Rate

    Received SBT Certification (Net-Zero Targets) / Recognized as a CDP Climate Change “A” Score Company                          Use of Electric-Furnace Steel in the Steel Frame of Dai-ichi Life Kyobashi Kino Terrace
    In November 2025, we received SBT certification (Net-Zero Targets) for our goal of achieving net-zero greenhouse              As part of our material circulation efforts, we are promoting the recycling use of wood in steel frames. Dai-ichi
    gas emissions across our entire value chain by fiscal year 2050. We have also been recognized as an A-rated                   Life Kyobashi Kino Terrace is a rental office building featuring a hybrid wood-frame structure characterized by a
    company—the highest rating—in the CDP 2025 Climate Change assessment.                                                         design that combines an open, airy exterior that conveys the warmth of wood with a spacious, comfortable inte-
                                                                                                                                  rior free of columns. To balance architectural design with environmental performance, we used electric furnace
                                                                                                                                  steel—a recycled material typically employed for secondary materials such as rebar—in 70 percent of the main
                                                                                                                                  steel structural members. As a result, we succeeded in reducing CO2 emissions by 17.5 percent compared to
                                                                                                                                  using only conventional blast furnace steel. Combining the use of the hybrid wood-frame structure with the CO2
    Click here for details    Decarbonization
                                                                                                                                  sequestration provided by the wood, we achieved a CO2 reduction of approximately 37.5% compared to a steel
                                                                                                                                  frame construction office building of the same size.

              Innovation




                                                NOVARE is a co-creation platform designed to accelerate Shimizu’s
                                                transformation into a Smart Innovation Company.
                                                Three years since its establishment. NOVARE has organically connected its five locations (facilities) while fostering internal talent through its Corporate Venturing (CV) system and talent
                                                development programs, and promoting collaboration with external partners through business co-creation programs and Corporate Venture Capital (CVC). Furthermore, through ongoing
                                                dialogue with a diverse range of domestic and international partners, we have steadily achieved results such as the creation of new businesses and technologies, as well as the growth of
                                                our talent. We believe that the fusion of diverse knowledge, technologies, experiences,
                                                and aspirations—both within and outside the company—is the very source of new                  Click below for details on each facility
    Katsuyoshi Yuhara                           value creation. This cycle—in which strengths cultivated internally merge with external
    Executive Officer,                          insights, and the resulting outcomes are reinvested both inside and outside the com-
    Executive Conductor, NOVARE
                                                pany—is the driving force behind sustainable value creation. Through co-creation with
                                                diverse stakeholders—including various internal divisions, as well as clients, partner
                                                companies, universities and research institutions, startups, and local communities—we
                                                continue to tackle social challenges and create new business opportunities.
                                                Our vision of “Choukensetsu” is a challenge to create new value by addressing the                                                                                                          Former
                                                fundamental needs of society and our clients, building on the technology and trust we                                                                                                      Shibusawa
                                                have cultivated over the years. NOVARE aims to create not merely a gathering place for                                                                                                     Residence
                                                people and knowledge, but a co-creation ecosystem where diverse stakeholders—both
                                                inside and outside the company—are organically connected, allowing new value to be
                                                continuously generated. By transcending boundaries and eventually eliminating them
                                                altogether, we foster a cycle in which diverse knowledge and aspirations converge.
                                                NOVARE will continue to serve as the starting point for a co-creation ecosystem that
                                                generates both social and corporate value.

        Q&A with the Chairman of the Board of Directors and Outside Directors
        As part of our efforts to sustain the company’s business growth and build its overall value, we commit to
        rigorous corporate governance. At the core of our corporate governance system is the Board of Directors.                                          What is your impression of the company’s Board of Directors?
        Below is a conversation between Kazuyuki Inoue (Chairman of the Board of Directors and Representative
        Director) and outside directors about the board’s current performance and future prospects.                                           The meeting agenda is well planned out by the executive team. It is high in quality, and clear on the key
                                                                                                                                              issues and risks. Much of this is due to the company’s robust decision-making process, which is grounded
                                                                                                                                              in the credo, The Analects and the Abacus. Even as an outside director, I feel comfortable in expressing
                                                                                                                                              concerns or offering suggestions. There is just the right amount of healthy tension but also an atmosphere
                                                                                                                                              of trust. I believe that the board can become even more effective with more in-depth strategic discussions
                                                                                                                                              for sustaining the company’s growth and building its overall value.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         Junichi Kawada

                                                                                                                                              The board has an atmosphere where every board member can earnestly consider and exchange ideas about
                                                                                                                                              how the company can sustain its growth and maximize profits over the medium and long term. Every year,
                                                                                                                                              board discussions go into more frequent and greater depths on the Mid-Term Business Plan and strategies
                                                                                                                                              for each business area. In the event of a dispute or an accident, outside directors, along with members of the
                                                                                                                                              Audit & Supervisory Board, exercise our supervisory function, playing an active role in discerning the facts
                                                                                                                                              of the case and making sure that the necessary action is being taken. Because this is a year for formulating
                                                                                                                                              the next Mid-Term Business Plan, which will begin next year, I expect the board to devote more time to
                                                 Yoshio Kometani                            Junichi Kawada                                    discussing the future of Shimizu. I would add that the board’s staff team is very conscientious in responding
                                                 Director                                   Director
                                 Mayumi Tamura                  Kazuyuki Inoue                           Yumiko Jozuka                        to our feedback.

                      What are your thoughts on the process for investment decisions?
        When it comes to investment decisions, our role as outside directors is not just to rein in executives’ pro-        The management is more likely to make the right decision about an investment proposal if it clarifies how
        posals, but also to support executives in taking calculated risks. It is therefore essential for management         the proposal fits into the company’s overall growth strategy and what the expected return is, and if it ana-
        to fully disclose what the purpose of the investment is, what the risks are, how the risks will be managed,         lyzes the risks and prepares measures to manage them. Given that the board considers such matters from a
        and expected returns, so that the board can have a transparent and impartial discussion. With M&A and               variety of viewpoints, I feel that the board’s monitoring function is working. The business unit proposing the
        investments for growth, it is all the more important to subject the proposal to scrutiny from an outsider’s         investment and the board’s staff brief outside directors beforehand, enabling us to read up on the proposal
        perspective that is uninfluenced by company norms. Such a perspective helps the management make deci-               ahead of the meeting. I will continue to identify risks from my perspective as an outside director in order to
        sions that will ultimately increase corporate value over the medium and long term.                                  help the executive team take calculated risks.

    Governance

                                                                                                                                                                                                      Overview of the Corporate Governance Structure
       Initiatives to Improve Governance
                                                                                                                                                                                                      We have adopted the structure of a company with an Audit & Supervisory
                                                                                                                                                                                                      Board. We have chosen this system based on the belief that we have
    Based on our Credo, The Analects and the Abacus, we strive to fulfill social responsibilities through our business activities, thereby earning a greater degree
                                                                                                                                                                                                      established a system to supervise and oversee management from an ob-
    of trust from our shareholders and investors, and all other stakeholders, including clients, employees, and local communities. We also aim to manage the
                                                                                                                                                                                                      jective and neutral perspective. This was achieved by limiting the number
    company in an expeditious, efficient, transparent, and lawful manner to achieve sustainable growth and increase corporate value over the medium to long term.
                                                                                                                                                                                                      of directors and introducing an executive officer system to clearly separate
    To achieve this, we have established a structure that enables the Board of Directors and Audit & Supervisory Board Members to appropriately supervise
                                                                                                                                                                                                      the management strategy decision-making and oversight functions from
    and audit the performance of duties, based on separation of the management strategy decision-making function from the execution of duties function.
                                                                                                                                                                                                      the business execution function, by taking steps to encourage energetic
    Concurrently, our basic policy on corporate governance is for our directors, executive officers, audit & supervisory board members, and employees to
                                                                                                                                                                                                      debate at the Board of Directors, and by electing outside directors, other
    practice compliance management based on high ethical standards.

        Establishment and Operation Status of Internal Control System
                                                                                                                                                                                                 Risk Management
        We have established a system of internal controls and the Board of Directors makes decisions on the Basic Policy on Establishing a System of Internal
        Controls to ensure proper operation of the company.
                                                                                                                                                                                              The Shimizu Group works to ensure continuous and stable development
        An overview of the operational status of internal control systems in fiscal 2025 is provided below.

Anti-Bribery Regulations
In addition to explicitly prohibiting bribery in our Code of Corporate Eth-
ics, we have established a system to ensure strict compliance and have
enacted these Anti-Bribery Regulations with the aim of preventing, be-
fore they occur, acts of bribery by officers and employees, as well as the
provision of improper benefits to public officials and others.
Under these regulations, the Company prohibits officers and employees

Director Skills Matrix
The Corporation identified skills (knowledge, experience, and abilities) necessary to respond quickly to changes in the business environment and to achieve sustainable growth and medium- to long-term improvement in corporate value
as below. The Corporation aims to maintain the appropriate size and diversity of the Board of Directors and also to optimize the Board of Directors as a whole and maximize its functions by organically combining skills of each director.

                                                                        Based on our fundamental philosophy of social contribution, we actively engage with local communities both in Japan and overseas and carry out a variety of social contribution
Contributing to Society                                                 activities. Here, we introduce some of our social contribution initiatives.



Basic Principles

     Shimizu actively contributes to society and local communities as a corporate citizen. By doing so, we help preserve the abundance of the earth and create a future society in which all people on it can
     live with happiness. Our activities are rooted in The Analects and the Abacus (Ethics and Economics), which contains the basic principles for managing our business; our corporate slogan, Today’s Work,
     Tomorrow’s Heritage.
